- **i-ab**: Audit a merge commit for silently-discarded changes — the GSDL-715/720 failure mode where conflict resolution keeps stale branch content and reverts already-landed fixes. Standalone audit tool; /i-crpr orchestrates this automatically as part of PR creation. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i-ab.agent.md) · 5 copies)

- **i-cr**: DEPRECATED — use /i-code-reviewer. Conduct security-focused code review with production safety checklist: blast radius, rollback path, test isolation, Athena cost guards (LIMIT/partition WHERE), and OWASP Top 10. Use on every PR as the reviewer — not the author. The 400-line rule, IAM checks, and data safety audit are mandatory outputs. - **i-crpr**: Create a production-ready PR with mandatory merge regression pre-flight. Runs git merge --no-commit origin/main, audits the staged result with /i-ab --pending, offers to restore confirmed losses in-place before committing, then pushes and opens the PR with the audit summary embedded. Use instead of bare git merge + gh pr create — the primary guard against the GSDL-715/720 silent regression pattern. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/i-crpr.md) · 5 copies)

- **i2r-context-analyst**: I2R PHASE 2 specialist. Spawned by i2r-orchestrator to build 02-context.json — actors/personas, jobs-to-be-done, domain glossary, success metrics, and the constraint register — each grounded to a raw source_ref or an explicit assumption. May request search mode when the domain is unfamiliar. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-context-analyst.md))
- **i2r-evidence-researcher**: I2R phase 2.5 (conditional, only when routing requires search) — gathers source-grounded evidence into 02b-evidence.json: terminology, constraints, standards and comparable patterns. Search informs context and NEVER invents product scope or overrides user intent; every external fact carries a source reference. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-evidence-researcher.md))
- **i2r-functional-author**: I2R PHASE 4 specialist (runs in parallel with i2r-nfr-author). Spawned by i2r-orchestrator to convert each in-scope capability into atomic EARS functional requirements in 04-functional.json — one requirement = one behaviour, [CAT]-NN IDs, source-grounded, with zero implementation leakage. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-functional-author.md))
- **i2r-intake-clarifier**: I2R phase 1 — normalizes a raw idea into 01-intake.json, separating STATED from ASSUMED and DECISION, applying the Mom-Test fluff filter, building an assumption map, scoring ambiguity and listing blocking open questions. Never fabricates; never asks the user directly. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-intake-clarifier.md))
- **i2r-nfr-author**: I2R PHASE 4 specialist (runs in parallel with i2r-functional-author). Spawned by i2r-orchestrator to walk ISO/IEC 25010:2023 quality characteristics into 05-nfr.json — each required NFR carrying a measurable Volere fit_criterion (threshold + environment + period). No vague "fast/secure/scalable". ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-nfr-author.md))
- **i2r-orchestrator**: Lead of the I2R pipeline — turns a raw or messy idea into a reviewed Markdown-first requirements package. Asks the run language at the start, runs mode routing, dispatches the specialist i2r-* agents layer by layer, holds the two human gates, assembles the package, then runs the dual review and bounded repair loop before gating. Never authors requirements itself; never declares READY without passing gate.check. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-orchestrator.md))
- **i2r-scope-architect**: I2R PHASE 3 specialist — the anti-scope-creep firewall. Spawned by i2r-orchestrator to build 03-scope.json — in-scope / out-of-scope / deferred, a capability inventory, and a MoSCoW seed. A wrong scope poisons every downstream FR/NFR, so be explicit and conservative. ([SensLiao/Claude-code-setting](https://github.com/SensLiao/Claude-code-setting/blob/HEAD/agents/i2r-scope-architect.md))

- **icu-clinical-reasoner**: Use when an ICU clinical consult needs bedside clinical reasoning — a patient scenario (Mode A), a mechanism/physiology question (Mode B), or an antimicrobial-selection question (Mode C) — and you want the view of a board-certified pediatric intensivist running the case. Reasons independently from the clinical data and question alone; returns a structured internal note (clinical frame, differential/mechanism, key data, management, pitfalls, confidence) that the consult synthesizer consumes. Does not verify citations or surveillance numbers — those gate downstream. ([neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult](https://github.com/neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult/blob/HEAD/agents/icu-clinical-reasoner.md) · ★1)
- **icu-consult-synthesizer**: Use when the four independent ICU consult agents (clinical reasoner, guideline synthesizer, human pathophysiology, basic science) have each produced their internal note and you need them merged into one physiology-forward, guideline-aware, colleague-to-colleague consult. Runs the adversarial cross-check, writes the Mode A / B / C output, states convergence, and names every dose/threshold/% and citation for the orchestrator's fabrication-auditor and citation-verifier gates, which run after it. Returns the final chat response with a convergence indicator. ([neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult](https://github.com/neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult/blob/HEAD/agents/icu-consult-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **icu-guideline-synthesizer**: Use when an ICU clinical consult needs the clinical-practice-guideline and systematic-review layer — the view of an expert who lives in society recommendations (SCCM, AHA, ATS, ESPNIC, PALICC, Surviving Sepsis, ELSO, IDSA, SIS, WSES). Searches PubMed and society pages, extracts each recommendation with GRADE strength and year, and returns a structured internal note (guidelines found, key recommendations, conflicts, evidence gaps, outdated alerts) that the consult synthesizer consumes. Reasons independently from the clinical data alone. ([neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult](https://github.com/neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult/blob/HEAD/agents/icu-guideline-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **icu-pathophysiology-basic**: Use when an ICU clinical consult needs the animal-model and basic-science layer — the view of a bench scientist who thinks in molecular pathways, receptor pharmacology, inflammatory cascades, and model validity. Surfaces relevant animal-model and molecular-mechanism data, assesses translational validity, flags what bench data predicts but humans have not confirmed, and names emerging mechanisms not yet clinical. Returns a structured internal note that the consult synthesizer consumes. Reasons independently from the clinical data alone; basic-science evidence is the floor of the hierarchy. ([neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult](https://github.com/neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult/blob/HEAD/agents/icu-pathophysiology-basic.md) · ★1)
- **icu-pathophysiology-human**: Use when an ICU clinical consult needs the human-and-translational pathophysiology layer — the view of a physician-scientist who thinks in organ-system interactions, cellular mechanisms, and bedside-to-bench connections. Explains the mechanism from organ to cell, cites human studies under a strict pediatric-first evidence hierarchy, quantifies effect sizes and thresholds, and judges whether physiology aligns with guidelines. Returns a structured internal note that the consult synthesizer consumes. Reasons independently from the clinical data alone. ([neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult](https://github.com/neelshah4/claude-icu-clinical-consult/blob/HEAD/agents/icu-pathophysiology-human.md) · ★1)
